   try:\n            with self.state_lock:\n                names = list(msg.joint_names)\n                now_monotonic = time.monotonic()\n                gripper_requested = GRIPPER_JOINT in \"\"\"\u8bf7\u6c42\u54cd\u4e00\u58f0\uff1b\u4e32\u53e3\u7531\u672c\u8282\u70b9\u72ec\u5360\uff0c\u53ea\u80fd\u4ece\u8fd9\u91cc\u53d1\u51fa\u3002\"\"\"\n        if not self.board:\n            return\n
